Assistant2026-08-30T21:34:17.257Z

Rebuilt on your palette, same link: https://claude.ai/code/artifact/db848d95-b475-4f61-9984-a843c2ec9b09

On the cards. Each region is now one white sheet divided by hairline rules, not a grid of floating boxes. Results is a single sheet: verdict → totals → session details → cache behaviour → caveats. The reset list lost its tinted panel and is just a list between two rules. Shadows appear on exactly one thing, dialogs, so elevation always means modal.

Order now follows what you said matters. Verdict and dollar saving first, then a six-metric row directly beneath it — cache hit rate, cache read tokens, cache write tokens, input tokens, output tokens, error rate — then identification, then behaviour, then limitations. Recent sessions is its own sheet at the bottom of both results and home, and now carries hit rate and errors per row, not just the verdict.

Error rate is derivable, incidentally: the feasibility doc notes message.model == "<synthetic>" rows come from API error paths, so "2 of 50 failed, 4.0%" comes out of the parser for free. The sample session now has two, drawn in red on the timeline.

Type is IBM Plex Sans with IBM Plex Mono — engineered rather than fashionable, and every figure, path, model name and identifier is mono. Both open-licensed and self-hosted, so the CSP stays strict.

Color discipline, since you flagged AI-purple: each hue does exactly one job. Blue is interaction and the recommendation. Indigo appears in only two places — expiry ticks and the CHANGED badge. Green is money saved, amber is validation warnings, red is failed requests. No gradients, no glow, no animation.

Tailwind: the token sheet now ends with a mapping. #2563EB is blue-600, #6366F1 indigo-500, #059669 emerald-600, #D97706 amber-600, #DC2626 red-600, #64748B slate-500, #94A3B8 slate-400 — all stock. Only four neutrals need theme.extend, because your ground and ink are cooler and bluer than Tailwind's slate. So WP-07 can build this almost entirely in utility classes.

One flag: you picked "Dark instrument" but then specified a light palette, and I went light. Worth a look before I commit anything to docs/design/ — if the dark version is what you actually want, it's the same structure with the ramp inverted, not a redesign.

Assistant2026-08-30T21:38:23.943Z

Two things there — one I want to correct, then the restructure.

The "eleven pauses" number is computable locally: it's the count of timestamp deltas falling between 300s and 3600s, arithmetic on numbers already in the file. No model needed. What isn't local is generated prose — so the fix is that the sentence becomes a locale string with an interpolated count, which the i18n requirement wants anyway. That said, you're right that there's far too much of it. Cutting hard.

Same link: https://claude.ai/code/artifact/db848d95-b475-4f61-9984-a843c2ec9b09

Left nav. A 236px sidebar with Add session at the top, the session list below it, and the standing links pinned to the bottom. Each row carries id, project, chosen TTL and saving — enough to pick from at a glance, which let the separate "Recent sessions" table go away entirely. The active session is tinted rather than accent-barred. A session still being analyzed shows its progress bar right in its row, so it lands in the nav the moment you add it. Landing page shows the empty state.

Copy, cut hard. The results screen now carries exactly one explanatory sentence — "11 of 49 gaps fell between 5 minutes and 1 hour" — and everything else is label and number. The rates disclosure went from three sentences to one line with a why? link. Reset entries are 09:47 · req 22 — model opus-5 → sonnet-5. The three caveats are one line each. Resets and gap bands now sit side by side instead of stacking, which took a couple hundred pixels out of the page.

On the local-processing point: every number here is arithmetic on fields already in the file. Gap counts are timestamp deltas bucketed at 300s and 3600s; error rate is the share of <synthetic> rows, which the feasibility doc identifies as API error paths. What needed cutting was the writing around them, not the figures.

Mobile collapses the sidebar into a horizontal chip strip under the header, with Add as a compact primary button.

Two things still open before I commit anything to docs/design/: you picked dark and I built light, and the German copy is mine rather than a translator's. Say when you're happy and I'll export the screens and canvas link into docs/design/ so WP-07 and WP-08 have something to build against.
